We evaluate the core components on a prototype implementation, showing that opacity-based pruning outperforms scale-based pruning for progressive delivery. The source code is available at https://github.com/emanuele-artioli/MoQSplat.

</details>


### [140] [Transcribe, Then Reason: Two-Pass Decomposition for Multimodal Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18958)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 多模态模型单次调用长内容审查会"满足化"：约三分之一内容被丢弃、其余被美化；瓶颈在于生成的输出竞争而非感知；拆分为"先转录、再审查"两遍同权重推理可同时提升忠实度与覆盖率，但存在长度受限与脱离原文后虚构两类失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最自然的做法是把原始长录音或长文档直接交给多模态模型，一次调用完成审查。作者旨在指出这种单次调用会静默失败，并定位失败的真正原因，从而找到可行的替代方案。

Method: 通过对照实验排除可能原因：让同一模型直接转录以检验是否属于感知问题；用同文本的文字与图像对比以排除模态差异；给单次推理大幅增加推理预算以检验是否只是"想得不够"；最终提出"先转录、后审查转录稿"的两遍同权重分解方法，并在 21 个来源的测试集上与单遍基线对比。

Result: 单遍基线丢弃约三分之一内容并对其余部分进行美化；被丢弃内容在要求转录时几乎全部重现，说明并非感知失败。模态与更大推理预算均不能恢复丢失内容：额外预算被用于规划综述而非写下来源。转录—审查分解在 21 个来源上同时提升忠实度与覆盖率；收益不均匀，在单遍基线最弱处帮助最大，在基线已强处帮助很小，并与来源长度和模态相关。分解带来两类失败模式：审查阶段在超长来源上输出空间不足，以及脱离原文后凭记忆虚构。

Conclusion: 单遍长内容审查的瓶颈是生成负荷下输出预算被竞争，而非感知或思考深度。将任务拆为转录与审查两遍同权重调用，让每一步获得独立完整输出预算，是提升忠实度与覆盖率的有效方案；但需注意长来源的容量上限与失去原文接地后的虚构风险。

Abstract: The natural way to review a long recording or document with a multimodal model is to hand it the raw source and ask for a review in one call. We show that this quietly fails: the model satisfices, dropping roughly a third of the content and embellishing the rest. The failure is not perception--almost all of the dropped content reappears when the same model is simply asked to transcribe the source. The bottleneck is generation under load: a single pass cannot perceive, reason over, and write a long faithful review at the same time, because doing all three competes for one output. We rule out the obvious alternatives. It is not the modality: models read text and an image of the same text equally well. And it is not merely a matter of thinking harder: giving the single pass a far larger reasoning budget does not recover the lost content, because the model spends that budget planning a review rather than writing the source down. What works is to split the labor across two same-weights passes--first transcribe, then review the transcript--so each step gets a full output budget of its own. This transcribe-then-review decomposition improves both faithfulness and coverage across a 21-source suite. The benefit is not uniform: we observe that it helps most where the one-pass baseline is weakest and little where that baseline is already strong, a pattern that also tracks the source's length and modality. Decomposition comes with two failure modes--the review pass running out of room on very long sources, and confabulating from memory once the grounding source is removed.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [SpecReuse: Spectral Graph Reuse for Efficient Vision GNN Inference on FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17718)
*Isabella Bernhardt Eiliya,Anvitha Ramachandran,Dhruv Parikh,Viktor Prasanna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecReuse算法与配套FPGA加速器，通过谱描述子漂移检测实现图复用，缓解ViG加速中动态图像图构建（DIGC）的性能瓶颈，取得最高2.69倍端到端加速和约58–65%的单次推理能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ViG的FPGA加速中，动态图像图构建（DIGC）是主要性能瓶颈：它在每一层都通过不规则、访存密集的计算重建图连接。现有FPGA加速器虽对DIGC做了优化，但仍无条件执行该过程，导致重复的图重建成为持续存在的延迟与能耗来源。因此需要一种能够判定何时真正需要重建图、从而跳过冗余计算的方法。

Method: 提出SpecReuse算法：计算中间特征的紧凑谱描述子（spectral descriptors），当描述子漂移低于标定阈值时，复用此前已构建的图，从而避免重复的DIGC。同时提出SpecReuse加速器：一种FPGA架构，用轻量级硬件实现谱描述子提取与复用控制，并与现有图构建加速器保持兼容。

Result: 端到端推理最高获得2.69倍加速，单次推理能耗降低约58–65%，FPGA资源开销可忽略，分类精度损失极小。

Conclusion: 以谱描述子漂移为判据的图复用机制，能有效消除ViG加速中冗余的DIGC计算，在几乎不增加硬件成本与精度损失的前提下显著提升FPGA加速器的速度与能效，且可兼容现有图构建加速器。

Abstract: Dynamic Image Graph Construction (DIGC) is the primary performance bottleneck in FPGA acceleration of Vision Graph Neural Networks (ViGs), reconstructing graph connectivity at every layer through irregular, memory-intensive computation. Existing FPGA accelerators optimize DIGC but still execute it unconditionally, making repeated graph reconstruction a persistent source of latency and energy consumption. We propose the SpecReuse algorithm, which computes compact spectral descriptors of intermediate features and reuses previously constructed graphs when descriptor drift remains below a calibrated threshold. We further present the SpecReuse accelerator, an FPGA architecture that realizes graph reuse through lightweight hardware for spectral descriptor extraction and reuse control while remaining compatible with existing graph-construction accelerators. Experimental results demonstrate up to a $2.69\times$ speedup in end-to-end inference and approximately 58--65\% lower energy per inference with negligible FPGA resource overhead and minimal loss in classification accuracy.

</details>


### [142] [Large Language Model based air quality monitoring and localized alert generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17954)
*Ricardo Vieira,Luis Tavares,Kaylane Lima,Lucas De Souza,Arthur Poggy,João Lima,Vitor Pinheiro,Markus Endler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EnQyMo：面向室内空气质量的物联网中间件平台，结合BLE信标定位与LLM代理，自动解读法规与文献中的健康暴露阈值，向受污染区域用户推送个性化告警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内空气质量对健康的直接危害可达室外的五倍，可致头痛、疲劳、眼喉刺激，长期暴露还与呼吸系统、心脏疾病及部分癌症相关。然而现有监测设备与系统（多面向办公室与工作场所）大多为被动式，缺乏对个体暴露风险的主动识别与针对性告警。

Method: 提出EnQyMo平台，一种通用的物联网中间件，用于处理室内空间的多类空气质量传感器数据，并将其与用户/员工的健康暴露风险相关联。系统利用BLE信标与移动IoT中间件识别暴露于污染空气或高CO2环境的（智能手机）用户，仅向处于不健康空气条件位置的人员生成位置相关告警。核心是一个由多个大语言模型（LLM）构成的代理机制，可解读监管标准与科学文献，自动识别关键健康暴露水平。

Result: 摘要为平台介绍性描述，未给出具体实验数据或量化评估结果；所声称的能力包括多源传感器数据处理、基于BLE的用户暴露识别、位置特异性告警生成，以及通过LLM代理自动提取监管标准与文献中的关键暴露阈值。

Conclusion: EnQyMo将IoT传感与LLM驱动的标准解读相结合，把被动的室内空气质量监测转变为针对个体的主动健康风险提示，具有在办公与工作场所推广的潜力。

Abstract: Poor indoor air quality can cause up to five times more direct health problems to occupants than outdoor air. In particular, it may cause headaches, fatigue, eye/throat irritation, and long-time exposure is linked to respiratory and heart as well as some forms of cancer. Despite the importance of indoor health and well-being, most current monitoring devices and systems (usually for offices and workspaces) are passive. The Environmental Quality Monitor (EnQyMo) platform is a generic Internet of Things (IoT) middleware designed to process several sensor data related to air quality in indoor spaces and correlate this data with health exposure risks of users/workplace employees. Using Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) beacons and a mobile IoT middleware it is able to identify the (smartphone) users exposed to these polluted air or high CO2 (carbon dioxide) levels, and generate location-specific alarms only to the users at the places with the unhealthy air conditions. At the core of EnQyMo is an agency of Large Language Models (LLMs) capable of interpreting regulatory standards and scientific literature to automatically identify critical health exposure levels.

</details>


### [143] [Towards Training Private LLMs: Exploring Fine-Tuning Language Models on Apple Silicon with RDMA over Thunderbolt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18066)
*En-Ming Huang,Yao-Ting Hsieh,Hsiang-Yu Tsou,Mu-Chi Chen,Shih-Hao Hung,H. T. Kung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究 Apple Silicon 是否能作为私有 LLM 微调的实用平台，通过优化 RDMA-over-Thunderbolt 分布式通信，在四节点 Mac Studio 集群上提升微调吞吐，并与 NVIDIA H100 进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有 LLM 微调对使用敏感数据的组织日益重要，但常超出普通数据中心加速器的内存容量。Apple Silicon 凭借大统一内存、较低整机成本，以及近期通过 RDMA-over-Thunderbolt 支持分布式执行，提供了不同的设计思路，因此有必要研究其是否适合私有 LLM 微调。

Method: 作者首先刻画 Mac Studio 节点上的 RDMA-over-TB 通信，发现实测带宽远低于 TB 标称规格；随后扩展 Apple 的实现，引入多干线通信、持久化工作线程和 CPU 侧梯度重叠，以更好利用多条直连 TB 链路进行 LLM 微调。

Result: 在四节点 Mac Studio 集群上微调 Qwen3-9B 时，优化使弱扩展吞吐相比单干线、无重叠基线最高提升 1.6 倍，在序列长度 17408 下达到 936 tokens/s。与 NVIDIA H100 平台对比，量化了内存容量、吞吐和购置成本之间的权衡。

Conclusion: Apple Silicon 可为私有 LLM 微调提供一种具有成本效益的解决方案。

Abstract: Private large language model (LLM) fine-tuning is increasingly important for organizations that need to adapt models using sensitive data, but it often exceeds the memory capacity of commodity datacenter accelerators. Apple Silicon offers a different design point through large unified memory and lower complete-system cost, while recent Apple software support enables distributed execution over RDMA-over-Thunderbolt (TB). This paper studies whether Apple Silicon can serve as a practical platform for private LLM fine-tuning. We characterize RDMA-over-TB communication on Mac Studio nodes, showing that the measured bandwidth is far below nominal TB specifications. Next, we extend Apple's implementation with multi-trunk communication, persistent worker threads, and CPU-side gradient overlap to better exploit multiple direct TB links for LLM fine-tuning workloads. Finally, on a four-node Mac Studio cluster that fine-tunes a Qwen3-9B, our optimizations improve weak-scaling throughput by up to 1.6X over the single-trunk, non-overlapped baseline and reach 936 tokens/s for sequence length 17408. We further compare Apple Silicon with an NVIDIA H100 platform to quantify the trade-off between memory capacity, throughput, and acquisition cost, showing that Apple Silicon can provide a cost-effective solution for private LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [144] [SSD-LLaMA: SSD-Native Inference for Trillion-Parameter MoE at 1+ Token/s on a Consumer PC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18110)
*Fangzhou Liang,Yibin Shen,Jianmin Hu,Jiayang Xu,Hanchi Gao,Minxian Xu,Zili Meng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SSD-LLaMA 是一个面向 SSD 的本地 MoE 推理系统，通过 SSD I/O 流水线、三级存储层次和 CPU-GPU 混合执行，在消费级硬件上高效运行前沿大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿开源权重语言模型采用 MoE 架构扩大容量，但本地推理需保留完整专家池，即便量化后仍远超消费级 RAM 与 VRAM 容量。SSD 虽提供足够容量，却需解决专家高效交付、SSD/RAM/VRAM 协同管理以及在受限带宽下的 CPU-GPU 混合执行问题。

Method: SSD-LLaMA 提出三项核心设计：面向专家交付优化的 SSD I/O 流水线；原生三级存储层次，动态交付并保留专家；均衡的 CPU-GPU 混合执行。系统对每个被选中的专家都完整执行，不做剪枝或替换。

Result: 在三个前沿 MoE 模型家族上，SSD-LLaMA 相对基线将预填充 token 速率提升 1.52×–4.19×，解码 token 速率提升 2.10×–15.58×。使用单张 RTX 5090 且不超过 32GB RAM，可运行万亿参数模型并达到高于 1 token/s。

Conclusion: SSD-LLaMA 证明了以 SSD 为原生存储、结合三级存储管理与 CPU-GPU 混合执行，可在消费级硬件上高效运行超大规模 MoE 模型，显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Frontier open-weight language models increasingly use Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures to expand model capacity while activating only a small subset of experts per token. Local inference must nevertheless keep the complete expert pool available, which remains far beyond consumer-grade RAM and VRAM capacity even after quantization. SSDs provide practical capacity at this scale, but turning that capacity into executable model memory requires efficient expert delivery, coordinated management of SSD, RAM, and VRAM, and CPU--GPU hybrid execution under bounded bandwidth. We present \textit{SSD-LLaMA}, an SSD-native local MoE inference system that addresses these challenges with an SSD I/O pipeline optimized for expert delivery, a native three-tier storage hierarchy that delivers and retains experts dynamically, and balanced CPU--GPU hybrid execution. \textit{SSD-LLaMA} executes every selected expert without pruning or substitution. Across three frontier MoE model families, \textit{SSD-LLaMA} improves prefill token rate by 1.52$\times$--4.19$\times$ and decode token rate by 2.10$\times$--15.58$\times$ over the evaluated baselines. We also achieve higher than 1 token/s for running trillion-parameter model with a single RTX 5090 and no more than 32GB RAM.

</details>


### [145] [Token Latency Fairness: Performance Isolation for Multi-Tenant LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18112)
*Dev Bali,Soujanya Ponnapalli,Yichuan Wang,Natacha Crooks,Scott Shenker,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 FairInference，为多租户 LLM 服务提供 δ-token 公平性保证，将行为良好客户端的 token 延迟上界限制为隔离时的 d+δ，同时提升整体吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务通常为共享多租户模式，高需求客户端的负载会导致其他客户端的延迟 SLO 违规。现有性能隔离方案仅在长期平均意义上均衡吞吐（如队列与批处理公平），无法提供延迟隔离保证，行为良好的客户端仍会遭遇显著的 token 级延迟退化。

Method: FairInference 提出 δ-token 公平性保证，核心挑战是在缺乏细粒度调度与资源分配支持的 GPU 共享下界定延迟。其调度器为每个 token 强制截止时间，限制 GPU 计算共享带来的延迟，并计入共享 KV 缓存引入的额外延迟。

Result: FairInference 能有效限制行为良好客户端的 token 级延迟尖峰，相比当前最先进的 LLM 服务系统提升了整体吞吐。

Conclusion: FairInference 通过 δ-token 公平性保证实现了较强的延迟隔离，在保证行为良好客户端 token 延迟上界的同时兼顾吞吐效率，优于现有 LLM 服务系统。

Abstract: LLM serving is typically offered as a shared, multi-tenant service, where high-demand workloads from one client can cause latency SLO violations for others. Existing solutions for performance isolation equalize client throughput in the long run, for example through queueing and batching fairness. However, these approaches do not provide latency isolation guarantees; as a result, well-behaved clients can still experience significant degradation to their token-level latencies.
  In this paper, we present FairInference, which provides the novel δ-token fairness guarantee: for a well-behaved client, if a token is generated in d time units in isolation, it will be generated within d + δ time units in multi-tenant execution, providing strong latency isolation guarantees for LLM serving. To achieve this, FairInference addresses a key challenge of LLM serving: bounding delays from sharing GPU resources without support for fine-grained scheduling or resource allocation. In FairInference, the scheduler enforces per-token deadlines, while bounding the delays from GPU compute sharing and accounting for the additional delays introduced by the shared KV caching in GPU memory. We show that FairInference effectively bounds token-level latency spikes for well-behaved clients and improves overall throughput compared to state-of-the-art LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [146] [Zero-I/O Fault Recovery for Sharded Deep Learning via Dynamic Framework Dependency Rebinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18178)
*Genlang Chen,Junyi Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AccelPact：面向分片分布式训练的零I/O内存内故障恢复并行运行时，通过引用重绑定机制避免checkpoint回滚，提升goodput且保持参数一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式模型训练常受瞬时网络故障中断，传统方案需中止全部进程并回滚到最新checkpoint。周期性checkpoint虽提供持久性，但频繁快照带来严重存储背压：在1.216B参数解码器上测得每次更新的异步checkpoint造成高达+656.7%延迟、32.5 GiB主机内存消耗及3.39 TB/小时存储流量。因此需消除该开销。

Method: 提出AccelPact并行运行时，实现分片分布式训练的零I/O内存内故障恢复。观察到已提交优化器步通信失败时设备内存未被污染，但PyTorch FSDP等框架在模块包装器与参数层级间缓存内部通信句柄导致无法继续。AccelPact通过非侵入式引用重绑定机制解决依赖失效，并由带外Gloo共识协议协调。

Result: 在16块NVIDIA RTX 5880 GPU上训练全参数Mistral-7B，AccelPact消除checkpoint重放，相比冷重启提升1.197x整体goodput，相比NVRx checkpoint恢复在checkpoint年龄5时提升1.194x，年龄18时提升1.698x。十次连续故障注入中，全部16个rank保持比特级一致参数状态，零数值漂移。在4至16 GPU集群拓扑上，引用重绑定为常数时间（0.493–0.518 ms）。直接操作原生通信器实例，无需修改应用代码，并避免torch.compile下的编译器图断裂。

Conclusion: AccelPact为弹性深度学习提供高效基础，在不修改应用代码的前提下实现内存内故障恢复，提升训练吞吐并保证数值一致性。

Abstract: Distributed model training at scale is frequently interrupted by transient network failures, conventionally forcing cluster managers to abort all processes and roll back to the latest checkpoint. While periodic checkpointing provides durability, frequent snapshotting introduces severe storage backpressure: our measurements on a 1.216B-parameter decoder reveal that per-update asynchronous checkpointing incurs up to a +656.7% latency overhead, consumes 32.5 GiB of host memory, and generates 3.39 TB/hour of storage traffic. To eliminate this overhead, we present AccelPact, a parallel runtime enabling zero-I/O in-memory fault recovery for sharded distributed training. When communication fails at a committed optimizer step, device memory remains quiescent and uncorrupted, yet continuation fails because frameworks like PyTorch FSDP cache internal communication handles across module wrappers and parameter hierarchies. AccelPact resolves this dependency invalidation via a non-invasive reference-rebinding mechanism coordinated by an out-of-band Gloo consensus protocol. On 16 NVIDIA RTX 5880 GPUs training full-parameter Mistral-7B, AccelPact eliminates checkpoint replay, yielding a 1.197x whole-run goodput improvement over cold restart and 1.194x over NVRx checkpoint restoration at checkpoint age 5, rising to 1.698x at age 18. Across ten successive fault injections, all 16 ranks maintain bit-identical parameter state with zero numerical drift. Across 4-to-16 GPU cluster topologies, reference rebinding executes in constant time (0.493-0.518 ms). Operating directly on native communicator instances, AccelPact requires zero application-code modifications and avoids compiler graph breaks under torch.compile, providing an efficient foundation for resilient deep learning.

</details>


### [147] [GeoMesh: Workload-Balanced and Sign-Compressed Geo-Distributed LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18388)
*Changyong Shin,Jaerim Park,Minchul Kang,Younghun Go,Zhixiong Niu,Yongqiang Xiong,Gyeongsik Yang,Chuck Yoo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GeoMesh是一个面向异构GPU的同步式地理分布式训练框架，通过负载均衡和通信压缩解决慢速WAN与GPU性能差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨区域GPU训练面临GPU速度与显存差异大、WAN通信慢的挑战。同步方法虽更新稳定，但快速GPU需等待慢速GPU（占运行时最多20.9%），且所有worker平均65.8%的时间用于同步；异步方法虽减少等待，却因陈旧更新导致精度下降。

Method: GeoMesh为每块GPU分配合适的批大小与内层步数以实现每worker负载均衡，让快速GPU做更多有效工作而非等待；同时通过交换压缩的基于符号的伪梯度（附带轻量幅度和token计数）将通信量减少近32倍。

Result: 在异构GPU与Azure衍生的WAN环境下，GeoMesh相比代表性基线将达标困惑度所需时间最多减少70.2%，将掉队者和WAN导致的GPU空闲分别最多降低8.0倍和5.6倍，同时保持可比的零样本准确率。

Conclusion: GeoMesh通过同步框架下的负载均衡与通信压缩，有效缓解了异构GPU地理分布式训练中的等待与通信瓶颈，在不损失精度的前提下显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained on GPUs distributed across multiple regions, but geo-distributed training is challenging in practice. Real clusters often contain GPUs with different speeds and memory capacities, and they communicate over slow wide-area networks. Our analysis shows that this creates serious problems: existing synchronous methods preserve stable updates, but fast GPUs wait up to 20.9% of their runtime for slower ones, and all workers spend, on average, 65.8% of their runtime on synchronization. Recent asynchronous methods reduce waiting time but worsen the model accuracy due to stale updates. To address the problems, we present GeoMesh, a synchronous geo-distributed training framework for heterogeneous GPUs. GeoMesh balances per-worker workloads by assigning each GPU a suitable batch size and number of inner steps, so faster GPUs do more useful work instead of waiting. It also reduces communication volume by nearly 32x by exchanging compressed sign-based pseudo-gradients with lightweight magnitude and token count. Across heterogeneous GPUs and Azure-derived WAN, GeoMesh reduces time-to-target perplexity by up to 70.2% over representative baselines and lowers straggler- and WAN-induced GPU idle by up to 8.0x and 5.6x, respectively, while preserving comparable zero-shot accuracy.

</details>


### [148] [From Pixels to Semantics: Edge AI for UAV-Based Critical Infrastructure Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18448)
*Reza Farahani,Naser Hossein Motlagh,Zoha Azimi,Christian Timmerer,Lorenzo Carnevale,Sasu Tarkoma,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探讨无人机基础设施巡检从云端离线处理、边缘轻量目标检测，迈向由轻量视觉语言模型（VLM）驱动的全边缘原生语义巡检，并在Jetson硬件上验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁、隧道、大坝、电网等关键基础设施需要及时、可扩展的巡检。传统人工巡检成本高且危险，而现有无人机巡检虽已从云端离线处理演进到基于YOLO等轻量检测器的边缘实时缺陷定位，但仍局限于目标检测层面，缺乏对结构的上下文语义理解。因此，作者希望推动无人机巡检向语义化、边缘原生的方向发展。

Method: 文章对现有无人机巡检架构进行分类，梳理其系统挑战与架构需求；并在NVIDIA Jetson级别的无人机硬件上，使用COCO-Bridge数据集进行实验评估，集成微调后的YOLO-26M进行目标定位，以及轻量级SmolVLM-256进行语义推理，以验证语义边缘智能的可行性。

Result: 实验在Jetson无人机级硬件上评估了YOLO-26M目标定位与SmolVLM-256语义推理组合的可行性，验证了在边缘设备上实现语义智能巡检的潜力，并据此提出了面向智能体化、自主、可信及协同语义无人机巡检的未来方向。

Conclusion: 无人机基础设施巡检正从云端中心化处理与边缘目标检测，向由轻量VLM驱动的全边缘原生语义巡检转型；未来将沿边缘-云连续体发展出智能体化、自主、可信与协同的语义巡检体系。

Abstract: Critical infrastructure assets such as bridges, tunnels, dams, and power line networks require timely and scalable inspection. While conventional manual inspection remains costly and hazardous, unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based inspection has emerged as an efficient alternative for monitoring difficult-to-access structures. Existing UAV inspection pipelines have evolved from cloud-centric offline processing toward edge-based perception using lightweight object detectors such as YOLO for real- time defect localization. This article explores the transition toward fully edge-native semantic inspection powered by lightweight vision language models (VLMs), where UAVs move beyond object detection toward contextual structural understanding. It categorizes existing UAV inspection architectures, identifies their key system challenges and architectural requirements, and experimentally assesses the feasibility of semantic edge intelligence on NVIDIA Jetson UAV-class hardware using the COCO-Bridge dataset. The evaluation integrates a fine-tuned YOLO-26M for object localization and a lightweight SmolVLM-256 for semantic reasoning. Finally, it outlines future directions toward agentic, autonomous, trustworthy, and collaborative semantic UAV inspection across the edge-cloud continuum.

</details>


### [149] [DiverseFT: Diversifying Fault-Tolerant Systems using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18512)
*Arne Vogel,Christian Berger,Rüdiger Kapitza*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 DiverseFT，利用大语言模型基于参考实现自动、可扩展地生成可编译、可通过测试且在语义/二进制层面存在差异的代码，以降低复制协议实现多样化的成本，并用 PBFT、HotStuff、Raft 验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错一致性协议若各副本共享同一实现缺陷，且同时受影响的副本数超过可容忍阈值，协议即失效。理想情况下副本应独立失效，可通过多样化实现达成，但现实中所有副本通常共享同一实现，因为提供多个多样化实现困难且极其耗时。共享实现因其复杂性极易成为共性缺陷的高风险来源。

Method: DiverseFT 以参考实现为输入，利用大语言模型自动、可扩展地生成多样化代码，要求生成代码可编译、能通过测试，并在语义或二进制层面与参考实现存在差异，从而可替换参考实现中的相应代码，显著降低多样化成本。

Result: 对三种复制协议实现 PBFT、HotStuff 和 Raft 进行多样化验证，结果显示最多可对 65% 的代码库实现多样化，证明了使用大语言模型对复制协议实现进行多样化的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，借助大语言模型可以低成本、可扩展地实现复制协议实现的代码多样化，为提升容错一致性协议的独立失效能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant agreement protocols fail if replicas share a common flaw that simultaneously affects more replicas than the tolerable threshold. Therefore replicas should ideally fail independently, which can be achieved through diversification. However, in practice, often the same protocol implementation is shared by all replicas which is not surprising given that the provision of multiple diverse implementations is difficult and highly laborious. This poses a major risk, as a shared protocol implementation is a prime candidate for common bugs due to its complexity. With DiverseFT, we demonstrate how, given a reference implementation, Large Language Models (LLMs) can be utilised for the automated and scalable generation of code that compiles, passes tests, and crucially differs semantically/binary-wise, that can re- place code in the reference implementation, thereby significantly reducing diversification costs. We demonstrate the feasibility of diversification of replication protocol implementations using LLMs by diversifying three implementations: PBFT, HotStuff, and Raft, showing how up to 65% of the codebase can be diversified.

</details>


### [150] [COMPASS-ABS: Reducing Fragmentation in Shared GPU Clusters for Deep Learning Training Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18519)
*Yukai Zhou,Hongfan Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出与历史负载无关的碎片度量 SIF，并提出 COMPASS-ABS 调度算法将集群状态限制在锚定空间内，使 SIF 有界，从而提升资源利用率并缩短 DLT 作业完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享 GPU 集群中深度训练作业增多，资源碎片化导致集群利用率低、作业周转时间长。现有碎片度量在缺少负载分布信息时失效，且现有调度器无法持续将碎片维持在低水平。

Method: 首先提出 Scheduler-Induced Fragmentation (SIF)，一种基于部分节点概念、不依赖历史负载知识的度量。再提出 COMPASS-ABS，采用 COMPact-ASSured (COMPASS) 算法将集群状态限制在紧致的 Anchor-Based Space (ABS) 中，该空间构建充分利用主导负载规模与节点容量之间的拓扑对齐。并证明在符合理论与生产的负载组成条件下 SIF 被约束在 2/N 以内。

Result: 在物理集群和模拟集群上的评估表明，COMPASS-ABS 通过减少碎片化，有效提升资源利用率并降低 DLT 作业完成时间。

Conclusion: SIF 提供了不依赖历史信息的碎片度量，COMPASS-ABS 能持续将碎片控制在有界低水平，在实际与模拟环境中均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of deep learning technology, shared GPU clusters receive an increasing number of deep learning training (DLT) jobs. Yet resource fragmentation make such clusters underutilized and forces the DLT jobs running on them to endure long turnaround times. Extensive research has been devoted to quantifying fragmentation and developing scheduling algorithms that alleviate its impact. However, existing fragmentation measures break down in the absence of workload distribution information, while current schedulers cannot continuously maintain resource fragmentation at a low level. To tackle these problems, we first introduce Scheduler-Induced Fragmentation (SIF), a metric built on the notion of partial-nodes that is independent of historical workload knowledge. We then propose COMPASS-ABS, which employs the COMPact-ASSured (COMPASS) algorithm to confine the cluster state within a tight Anchor-Based Space (ABS), whose construction fully leverages the topological alignment between dominant workload size and node capacity. Moreover. We also prove that it ensures SIF is bounded by $\frac{2}{N}$ under a workload composition condition that matches both theory and production. Evaluations implemented on a physical cluster and a simulated cluster demonstrate COMPASS-ABS effectiveness at improving resource utilization, reducing DLT job completion time by reducing fragmentation.

</details>


### [151] [AUPE: Collaborative byzantine fault-tolerant peer-sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18563)
*Augusta Mukam,Joachim Bruneau-Queyreix,Laurent Réveillère*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 AUPE，首个利用可信节点（如 Intel SGX 设备）协作抗拜占庭的同伴采样协议，通过在系统中追踪标识符传播并在本地消除拜占庭节点的表示偏差，显著提升了大规模场景下的抗攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同伴采样是分布式系统的关键原语，用于管理覆盖网络和信息传播，在无许可区块链等大规模场景中尤为重要。它通过维护本地部分视图来反映系统成员。然而恶意节点常试图通过增加其在诚实节点视图中的比例，来操纵诚实节点对拜占庭节点存在的感知。现有最先进的拜占庭容错同伴采样协议虽能缓解该偏差，但随着恶意节点数量增加，其有效性显著下降。因此需要新的方案来提升高比例恶意节点下的容错能力。

Method: AUPE 是一个协作式拜占庭容错同伴采样协议。它利用可信节点（如具备 Intel SGX 能力的设备）的存在，协作追踪标识符在系统中的传播情况，并在本地对拜占庭节点的表示进行去偏处理。通过引入少量可信节点来协同检测和修正诚实节点视图中的偏差，从而提升整体协议的抗攻击能力。

Result: 在 10,000 个节点的仿真中，AUPE 优于现有最先进方案，即使面对控制 26% 节点的对手也能实现近乎完美的弹性。总体而言，仅引入 10% 的可信节点，AUPE 就能将 BRAHMS 的容忍度提升多达 60%，同时在对手持有高达 40% 节点的情况下仍能限制其攻击影响。

Conclusion: AUPE 是首个协作式拜占庭容错同伴采样协议，借助少量可信节点即可显著提升系统在高比例恶意节点下的抗攻击能力，在弹性和容忍度上均优于现有方案，为大规规模分布式系统（如无许可区块链）提供了更稳健的同伴采样机制。

Abstract: Peer sampling is a crucial primitive in distributed systems, used to manage overlays and disseminate information in large-scale scenarios such as permissionless blockchain systems. Its purpose is to maintain and regularly update a local and partial snapshot, or view, of the complete system's membership. These protocols are often targeted by malicious actors who aim to disrupt higher-level protocols. Typically, an adversary who controls a set of byzantine nodes attempts to manipulate how legitimate nodes perceive the presence of byzantine ones by increasing their representation in the view of honest nodes. While state-of-the-art byzantine-tolerant peer sampling protocols mitigate this bias, their effectiveness decreases significantly as the number of malicious nodes increases. This paper introduces AUPE, the first collaborative Byzantine-tolerant peer sampling protocol that leverages the presence of trusted nodes, such as Intel's SGX capable devices, to collaboratively track the spread of identifiers in the system and locally debias the representation of byzantine nodes. Simulations with 10,000 nodes demonstrate that AUPE outperforms state-of-the-art solutions, achieving nearperfect resilience even when faced with an adversary controlling 26% of the nodes. Overall, by including as few as 10% of trusted nodes, AUPE increases the tolerance of BRAHMS by up to 60% while limiting the impact of the adversary's attack, even when possessing up to 40% of the nodes.

</details>


### [152] [RayOrch: Programming and Executing Lineage-Controlled Multi-Grain Dataflows for Foundation-Model Data Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18703)
*Xiaochen Ma,Zimo Meng,Junzhu Liang,Youhe Jiang,Yue Cheng,Hao Liang,Bohan Zeng,Dengchun Li,Lu Ma,Zhengyang Zhao,Zhen Hao Wong,Runming He,Meiyi Qiang,Jiangtao Guan,Binhang Yuan,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RayOrch 是一个保留父子关系的编程模型与分布式执行引擎，用于高效处理可变基数、长尾分布的数据扩充流水线，在 NVIDIA H20 上显著加速 MinerU 与视频流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型训练数据准备需要可扩展流水线，将异构文档和视频转换为结构化记录。这类流水线会把每个父项扩展为有序、依赖输入的若干子项，子项数量可能呈长尾分布。GPU 需要在跨父项批处理子项的同时，保留父子关系、子项顺序、完成状态和结果路由。现有系统要么把并行隐藏在粗粒度作业中，要么暴露扁平记录，迫使应用自行管理血缘和重新分组。

Method: 论文提出 RayOrch，一种在全程执行中保留父子关系的编程模型与分布式执行引擎。程序声明有序、可变基数的扩展（expansion）及匹配的聚合（gather）；编译器验证每一对扩展与聚合，运行时记录子项成员关系、直接父项、不可变序数和终止状态。通过 Per-Call FIFO Ready Queues 跨父项批处理就绪子项，聚合依据声明的成员关系和序数重建结果，而非依赖批次边界或完成顺序。父项可在所有必需子项终止后立即推进；类型化的父作用域失败会抑制失败父项中未派发的兄弟子项，同时允许无关父项继续执行。

Result: 在 NVIDIA H20 GPU 上，RayOrch 将 MinerU 从 4 卡扩展到 64 卡时实现 15.14 倍加速，将视频流水线从 8 卡扩展到 64 卡时实现 7.82 倍加速。在 MinerU 上，端到端时间比 Ray Data 减少 13.1%，比 Daft 减少 29.0%；在 Docling 上比 Ray Data 减少 16.0%。代码已开源于 https://github.com/OpenDCAI/RayOrch。

Conclusion: RayOrch 通过保留父子关系的编程抽象与运行时机制，解决了可变基数、长尾子项分布下的批处理、血缘管理与容错问题，在多种数据准备流水线上实现了显著加速与端到端时间降低。

Abstract: Preparing high quality training data for foundation models requires scalable pipelines that transform heterogeneous documents and videos into structured records. Such pipelines expand each parent item into an ordered and input dependent sequence of children, whose counts may be long tailed. GPUs should batch children across parents while preserving parent relationships, child order, completion status, and result routing. Existing systems either hide parallelism behind coarse grained jobs or expose flat records that force applications to manage lineage and regrouping. We present RayOrch, a programming model and distributed execution engine that preserves parent child relations throughout execution. Programs declare ordered variable cardinality expansions and matching gathers. The compiler validates each pair, while the runtime records child membership, immediate parents, immutable ordinals, and terminal states. Per Call FIFO Ready Queues batch ready children across parents. Gathers reconstruct results from declared membership and ordinals rather than batch boundaries or completion order. Parents can advance as soon as all required children become terminal. Typed parent scoped failures suppress undispatched siblings of the failed parent while allowing unrelated parents to continue. On NVIDIA H20 GPUs, RayOrch achieves 15.14 times speedup when scaling MinerU from 4 to 64 GPUs and 7.82 times speedup when scaling a video pipeline from 8 to 64 GPUs. It reduces end to end time by 13.1 percent versus Ray Data and 29.0 percent versus Daft on MinerU, and by 16.0 percent versus Ray Data on Docling. Code available at https://github.com/OpenDCAI/RayOrch .

</details>


### [153] [Vigil: Accountable Liveness against Selective Silence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18778)
*Jiawei Cheng,Huiping Sun,Rui Zhou,Jinjue Zhou,Zhong Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究选择性子沉默（selective silence）下的BFT问责问题，提出下界K_SI ≥ f+1，并设计Vigil协议匹配该下界，实验验证其高效性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BFT问责在安全性违规方面已有充分研究，近期工作也涵盖全局活性违规，但针对"接收者选择性沉默"这一攻击（敌手对部分诚实节点隐瞒消息、对其他人表现正常）尚无有效机制。此类攻击能阻止共识却逃避现有所有检测手段，因此需要系统性研究。

Method: 负面结果：证明针对至多f个诚实节点沉默的单一攻击者与诚实节点不可区分，导出沉默识别阈值下界K_SI ≥ f+1，且无反馈修复代价为Θ(n^3)。正面结果：提出Vigil——Tendermint变体，采用攻击自适应转发、位图交叉认证、基于core的成员管理和挑战-应答审计。

Result: Vigil匹配下界：无选择性沉默时每节点O(n)认证器（加Θ(n^2)位图元数据位）；中继开销与攻击宽度成正比（次阈值沉默每视图最多n^3/27次中继）；多数指控机制以可调韧性τ_A（K_SI=τ_A+1，最优τ_A=f）指控沉默节点。实验：2%丢包下，f+1指控门槛误控91.2%诚实节点，而多数门槛仅误控0.002%。

Conclusion: 选择性沉默的问责在理论上存在固有下界，Vigil通过自适应机制达到该下界，并在真实三区域WAN及与闭式解严格一致的模拟器中验证了各阈值与代价，同时精确刻画了残余次阈值griefing面并将识别扩展到x-部分同步。

Abstract: BFT accountability is well understood for safety violations, and recent work attributes global liveness violations; \emph{recipient-selective} silence remains unresolved. A selectively silent adversary withholds messages from some honest nodes while behaving correctly toward others. It can stall consensus yet evade every existing mechanism. We initiate a systematic study of accountability against selective silence. Negatively, a lone attacker silent toward at most $f$ honest nodes is indistinguishable from an honest node, yielding a universal lower bound $K_{\mathrm{SI}} \ge f{+}1$ on the \emph{silence identification threshold}; moreover, any feedback-free repair after a silence-induced violation costs $Θ(n^3)$. Positively, \textsc{Vigil}, a Tendermint variant, matches these bounds with attack-adaptive forwarding, via bitmap cross-attestation, core-based membership, and challenge--response auditing. It pays $O(n)$ authenticators per node when no selective silence occurs (plus $Θ(n^2)$ bitmap metadata bits per node), relays in proportion to the attack's width (sub-threshold silence can force up to $n^3/27$ relays per view, a cost we price exactly), and majority-accuses any node silent toward more than a tunable resilience $τ_A$ of honest peers ($K_{\mathrm{SI}} = τ_A{+}1$, optimal at $τ_A = f$). We also price the residual sub-threshold griefing surface exactly and extend identification to $x$-partial synchrony. Real-network experiments on a three-region WAN, together with a simulator held to exact equality with every closed form, confirm each threshold and cost: at $2\%$ loss, an $f{+}1$ accusation bar falsely accuses $91.2\%$ of honest nodes, while our majority bar accuses $0.002\%$.

</details>


### [154] [A Distributed Computing Framework for Satellite Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18839)
*Ezra Fielding,Clement Demazure,Guthemberg Silvestre,Felipe Alves Suana,Philippe Quéinnec*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出面向卫星集群的分布式计算框架，并首次实现基于CRDT的最终一致分布式状态服务，在66星仿真星座中验证其可扩展性与容错性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型卫星星座与分布式空间系统规模达数十至数百颗，传统地面集中式指挥控制无法扩展，需引入分布式计算以实现容错、自主的空间应用。

Method: 提出涵盖分布式状态、指挥控制与科学任务的概念性分布式计算框架；作为首步验证，采用无冲突复制数据类型（CRDT，具体为Last-Write-Wins Register的键值存储）设计并实现强最终一致分布式状态服务，并在GoNetEm仿真的66星星座中评估空间态势感知目录分发场景。

Result: CRDT方案将地面到卫星通信从66条消息降至每次更新仅1条上行；树遍历的总消息数与直接上行相当；并发更新下星间消息呈亚线性增长，因节点丢弃过期版本而进一步降低网络开销。

Conclusion: 基于CRDT的分布式状态可作为卫星集群应用的可扩展、容错基础，验证了该框架的可行性。

Abstract: The rise of large satellite constellations and Distributed Space Systems (DSS) demands generalized frameworks that enable fault-tolerant, autonomous distributed space applications. Conventional ground-centric command and control does not scale to systems of tens or hundreds of satellites, motivating the adoption of distributed computing. This paper introduces a conceptual distributed computing framework for satellite swarms, covering distributed state, command and control, and scientific mission. As a first validation step, a strongly eventually consistent distributed state service is designed and implemented using Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDT), specifically a Last-Write-Wins Register-based key-value store. The service is evaluated in the context of Space Situational Awareness catalog dissemination across a simulated 66-satellite constellation emulated with GoNetEm. Results show that the CRDT-based approach reduces ground-to-satellite communication from 66 messages to a single uplink per update, with tree traversal matching the total message count of direct uplink. Under concurrent updates, sub-linear inter-satellite message growth is observed as nodes discard outdated versions, further reducing network overhead. These results demonstrate the viability of a CRDT-based distributed state as a scalable and fault-tolerant foundation for satellite swarm applications.

</details>


### [155] [Ask the Tool, Don't Guess: Agent Tool Calls Hold Their Progress, and the Serving System Should Read It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18849)
*Yipeng Liu,Yingqiang Zhang,Feifei Li,Huanchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文指出工具调用前无法预知持续时间，提出让工具运行时显式报告进度，用于KV缓存决策，大幅提升预测精度并降低TTFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent请求大量时间在等待工具执行，其KV缓存持续占用GPU显存。现有服务系统在调用开始前根据工具名、历史、预声明时长或引擎占用率猜测工具运行时间，以此决定缓存保留、驱逐或恢复。但论文证明任何调用前的固定估计都无法预知其真实时长，甚至无法对调用正确排序。真正的答案在运行中的工具手里，却被agent栈和工具本身掩盖了。

Method: 提出让工具调用在执行过程中显式报告自身进度。对四个公开agent语料进行普查，发现大多数工具时间在揭示后存在可读信号，分两种强度：剩余工作量比例，或接近结束的准确信号。构建一个harness在不改变agent可见内容的情况下恢复该信号，且对benchmark得分无显著成本。在KV缓存决策点使用该进度信号，并通过少量hint接入生产引擎。

Result: 在KV缓存决策点，报告的进度比已发表的最佳预测器准确数倍到近一个数量级，且环境变化时仍保持准确。接入生产引擎后，工具调用后的p90首token时间（TTFT）相比LRU降低20.7%（仅HBM）和20.8%（HBM+DRAM），接近oracle。

Conclusion: 服务系统不应猜测工具能主动告知的信息。让工具运行时报告进度是一种低成本、高准确率的方式，可显著优化KV缓存管理和TTFT表现。

Abstract: An agentic request spends substantial wall-clock time waiting for tools, and its KV cache holds GPU memory the whole time. Serving systems decide whether that cache stays, leaves, or comes back by guessing how long the tool will run, from the tool's name, its history, a duration declared before the call, or the engine's own occupancy. We show that no estimate fixed before a call starts can know its duration, and such estimates may not even rank the calls. Meanwhile, the running tool already holds the answer, but the agent stack together with the tool silences it. We propose that tool calls report their progress explicitly while they run, and we measure what that takes. A census of four public agent corpora finds a readable signal in most tool time once it is revealed, in two strengths: a fraction of the work remaining, or an accurate signal that the end is near. A harness recovers it without changing what the agent sees, at no measurable cost to the agent's benchmark score. At the points where a KV cache decision is made, the reported progress is between several times and an order of magnitude more accurate than the best published predictors, and it stays accurate when the environment changes. Plugged into a production engine through a few small hints, it cuts the p90 time to first token (TTFT) after a tool call by 20.7% (HBM only) and 20.8% (HBM + DRAM) against LRU, close to an oracle. A serving system should not guess what its tools can tell it.

</details>


### [156] [Fluid Notarization: Verifiable Evolution of Concurrently Edited Structured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18886)
*Amos Brocco,Giuliano Gremlich,Roberto Guidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出“Fluid Notarization”范式，将文档演化过程本身而非孤立快照作为链上存证对象，基于JSON原生delta-CRDT（Melda）记录因果关联的演化制品标识，实现确定性收敛与可独立审计的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于区块链的文档存证采用面向快照的模型，每次修订作为独立状态上链。该方式对不可变制品有效，但在协作编辑场景下不足：并发修改会产生分叉版本，需在存证层之外进行协调；且细微改动也需生成并分发新快照。另一方面，CRDT等协作复制框架能确定性协调并发更新，却不天然提供贡献何时发布的、可独立验证的证据。因此需要一种兼顾收敛性与可审计性的存证新范式。

Method: 提出Fluid Notarization存证范式，以文档演化本身为存证对象：不再认证孤立状态，而是认证由JSON原生delta-CRDT生成的、具有因果关联的演化制品图。该模型构建于Melda之上，将文档变更表示为紧凑的、内容寻址的delta，并通过因果依赖连接。区块链存证被简化为记录这些演化制品的标识符，而同步、重构与冲突解决完全留在链下完成。

Result: 该架构结合了两类互补保证：CRDT提供的确定性收敛，以及区块链提供的可独立审计的存在性证明、来源追溯与发布证据。基于协作编辑电子健康记录的原型实现与验证场景，证明了该方法的可行性，并凸显了存证文档演化而非连续快照的优势。

Conclusion: Fluid Notarization将存证重心从状态快照转向演化制品图，在保持链下同步与冲突解决效率的同时，借助区块链获得可审计的存在性、来源与发布证明，并通过电子健康记录场景验证了可行性，表明存证文档演化优于存证连续快照。

Abstract: Traditional blockchain-based document notarization follows a snapshot-oriented model in which each document revision is represented as an independent state anchored on-chain through a cryptographic reference. While effective for immutable artifacts, this approach becomes inadequate when documents evolve through collaborative editing. Concurrent modifications create divergent document versions that must be reconciled outside the notarization layer, while even minor changes require generating and distributing new document snapshots. Conversely, collaborative replication frameworks such as CRDTs provide deterministic reconciliation of concurrent updates, but do not inherently provide independently verifiable evidence of when contributions were published. This paper introduces Fluid Notarization, a notarization paradigm in which document evolution itself becomes the object of notarization. Rather than certifying isolated states, Fluid Notarization certifies a graph of causally related evolution artifacts generated by a JSON-native delta-CRDT. The proposed model builds upon Melda, which represents document changes as compact, content-addressed deltas linked through causal dependencies. Blockchain notarization is reduced to recording identifiers of these evolution artifacts, while synchronization, reconstruction, and conflict resolution remain entirely off-chain. The resulting architecture combines two complementary guarantees: deterministic convergence provided by the CRDT and independently auditable proof-of-existence, provenance, and publication evidence provided by the blockchain. A prototype implementation and validation scenario based on collaboratively edited electronic health records demonstrate the feasibility of the approach and highlight the advantages of notarizing document evolution rather than successive document snapshots.

</details>


### [157] [Ermes: a Stateful Serverless Platform for the Edge-to-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18924)
*Matteo Cenzato,Dario d'Abate,Arianna Dragoni,Giacomo Orsenigo,Luca Tosetti,Matteo Briscini,Alessandro Margara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ermes，一个将状态管理原生集成到 FaaS 范式中的分布式平台，通过集合（collections）组织应用状态并联合调度函数与状态，实现边缘到云连续体上的低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FaaS 的无状态特性迫使函数从外部（通常以云为中心）的数据存储中获取状态，重新引入了边缘计算本欲消除的延迟；同时，位置无关的调度器和僵化的一刀切一致性模型无法满足边缘应用的多样需求。

Method: Ermes 将应用状态组织为逻辑单元（collections），采用分布式协调算法将集合与函数联合映射到可用节点以最小化客户端感知延迟；并支持细粒度复制和从顺序一致性到最终一致性的按集合一致性级别。

Result: 实验评估表明，Ermes 能快速将远程状态访问转变为本地访问，并在工作负载增长及客户端在边缘间移动时保持低延迟。

Conclusion: 将状态管理原生集成进 FaaS 并联合调度计算与状态，可有效消除无状态带来的外部状态访问延迟，并通过灵活的一致性选择平衡一致性与性能。

Abstract: Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) is a widely adopted paradigm to simplify application deployment across the edge-to-cloud continuum. However, its stateless nature forces functions to retrieve their state from external, typically cloud-centric, data stores, reintroducing the very latency that edge computing aims to eliminate. This issue is further exacerbated by location-agnostic schedulers and rigid, one-size-fits-all consistency models that fail to capture the diverse requirements of edge applications. In this paper, we propose Ermes, a distributed platform that natively integrates state management into the FaaS paradigm, enabling the joint distribution of computational workloads and application state across the edge-to-cloud continuum. Ermes organizes application state into logical units, termed collections, and employs a distributed coordination algorithm that jointly maps collections and functions onto the available nodes seeking to minimize the latency perceived by the clients. In addition, it supports fine-grained replication and per-collection consistency levels, ranging from sequential to eventual consistency, leaving developers the choice of how to resolve the trade-off between consistency and performance. The experimental evaluation shows that Ermes quickly turns remote state accesses into local ones and sustains low latency as the workload grows, and as clients move across the edge.

</details>


### [158] [Replication-Aware Placement of Functions and Data in the Edge-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18932)
*Dario d'Abate,Matteo Cenzato,Matteo Briscini,Arianna Dragoni,Alessandro Margara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究FaaS中函数调度与数据放置的联合优化问题，在异构一致性要求下的数据复制场景中最小化客户端延迟，提出BLP最优模型及拓扑感知贪心启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FaaS已成为边缘-云连续体的主流编程模型，其将无状态函数与持久状态解耦。如何在考虑异构一致性要求下的数据复制时，联合调度函数与放置数据以最小化客户端延迟，是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 引入二元线性规划（BLP）模型计算最优放置，建立理论基准；由于BLP随基础设施节点数量立方级增长，进一步提出拓扑感知的贪心启发式算法以高效近似最优解。

Result: 评估表明，该启发式算法以极低的计算成本实现了接近最优的放置质量。

Conclusion: 该启发式方法适合用于周期性系统重配置，在保证质量的同时显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) has emerged as the prominent programming model for the edge-cloud continuum. FaaS inherently decouples stateless functions from their persistent state. We study how to jointly schedule functions and place data to minimize client latency, considering data replication under heterogeneous consistency requirements. We introduce a Binary Linear Programming (BLP) model to compute optimal placements, establishing a rigorous theoretical baseline. Since the BLP scales cubically with the infrastructure nodes, we propose a topology-aware greedy heuristic that efficiently approximates the optimal solution. Our evaluation shows that the heuristic achieves near-optimal placement quality at a fraction of the computational cost, making it suitable for periodic system reconfigurations.

</details>


### [159] [OAK: Restart- and Age-Aware Scheduling for Distributed Machine Learning on Shared GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19024)
*Khaled Aljbab,Amine Barrak*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OAK 是一种面向多租户 GPU 集群的每轮混合整数线性调度器，通过在 goodput 效用中引入"年龄键"和"分解式重启因子"两个一等项，在故障场景下显著降低作业完成时间，同时保持低决策延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式机器学习越来越多运行在共享的多租户 GPU 集群上，竞争与故障是常态。现有 goodput 驱动调度器虽最大化瞬时吞吐，但把累积等待时间和重启成本当作次要信号：长期等待的作业被反复推迟，且每次中断后用于加载检查点的数分钟开销未纳入分配决策。因此需要一个能同时显式建模等待老龄与重启开销的调度方案。

Method: 提出 OAK：一种每轮（per-round）混合整数线性（MILP）调度器。其复合效用函数在 goodput 基础上加入两个一等项：(1) 年龄键（age key），提升累积等待时间高的作业优先级；(2) 分解式重启因子（decomposed restart factor），将有效训练时间与检查点开销作为两个独立测量的量，而非单一聚合比率。

Result: 在 12-GPU 集群的 trace 驱动模拟器上，对四种数据并行工作负载、受控泊松故障注入，与四个代表性 goodput 与公平性驱动基线对比，并在 4-V100 真实硬件集群上复现故障模式改进。无故障时 OAK 与最强 goodput 基线差距在 4% 以内；λ=0.1 故障率下，相对其 goodput 驱动基础，平均 JCT 降低 33.9–58.3%，最坏情况 JCT 降低 58–76%；仅分解式重启因子一项即带来平均 40–57%、尾部 52–76% 的 JCT 改进（对比先前 goodput 调度器使用的聚合重启估计器）。每轮决策延迟仅 7.65 ms，比默认设置下的演化搜索类方法快三个数量级。

Conclusion: OAK 证明：将累积等待时间与分解式重启开销提升为 goodput 调度中的一等信号，可在几乎不损失无故障吞吐的前提下，大幅改善故障场景下的平均与尾部 JCT，且保持毫秒级、远优于演化搜索的决策延迟，适用于多租户 GPU 集群的实际调度。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning increasingly runs on shared multi-tenant GPU clusters where contention and failures are routine. Goodput-driven schedulers maximise instantaneous throughput but treat accumulated waiting time and restart cost as second-class signals: long-waiting jobs are repeatedly deferred, and the minutes of checkpoint loading paid after each interruption are not folded back into allocation decisions.
  We present OAK, a per-round mixed-integer linear scheduler whose composite utility adds two first-class terms to goodput: an age key that elevates jobs with high cumulative waiting time, and a decomposed restart factor that tracks productive training time and checkpoint overhead as separately measured quantities rather than a single aggregate ratio.
  We evaluate OAK against four representative goodput- and fairness-driven baselines in a trace-driven simulator on a 12-GPU cluster across four data-parallel workloads with controlled Poisson failure injection, and reproduce the failure-mode improvement on a 4-V100 real-hardware cluster.
  Under failure-free conditions OAK matches the strongest goodput-driven baseline within 4%. Under failures at rate $λ= 0.1$ it reduces mean job completion time (JCT) by 33.9-58.3% and worst-case JCT by 58-76% over its goodput-driven foundation; the decomposed restart factor alone accounts for a 40-57% mean and 52-76% tail JCT improvement against an aggregate restart estimator used in prior goodput-driven schedulers. Per-round decision latency is 7.65 ms, three orders of magnitude faster than evolutionary-search alternatives at default settings.

</details>
